focus_tree = {
	id = generic_focus_improved
	
	country = {
		factor = 5
	}
	
	default = yes
	reset_on_civilwar = yes

	initial_show_position = {
		focus = generic_industrial_effort
	}

	continuous_focus_position = { x = 50 y = 1100 }

	shared_focus = HABSBURG_part_of_something_bigger

	#Focus Path shortcuts	
	shortcut = {
		name = generic_politics_shortcut
		target = political_sphere 
	}

	shortcut = {
		name = generic_industry_shortcut
		target = generic_industrial_effort
	}
	shortcut = {
		name = generic_navy_shortcut
		target = generic_naval_effort 
	}
	shortcut = {
		name = generic_airforce_shortcut
		target = generic_aviation_effort 
	}
	shortcut = {
		name = generic_army_shortcut
		target = generic_army_effort
	}

### Army Focuses

	#Army Effort
	focus = {
		id = generic_army_effort
		icon = GFX_goal_generic_allies_build_infantry
		x = -10
		y = 0
		cost = 5
		search_filters = {  FOCUS_FILTER_ARMY_XP }
		
		available_if_capitulated = yes

		completion_reward = {
			army_experience = 15
			custom_effect_tooltip = generic_skip_one_line_tt
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_dynamic_modifier = generic_army_spirit_red_dynamic
				}
				custom_effect_tooltip = GENERIC_modify_army_red_modifier_tt
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					has_dynamic_modifier = generic_army_spirit_revolutionary_dynamic
				}
				custom_effect_tooltip = GENERIC_modify_army_rev_modifier_tt
			}
			else = {
				add_dynamic_modifier = {
				    modifier = generic_army_spirit_dynamic
				}
			}
			add_to_variable = { 
				army_spirit_experience_gain_army_factor = 0.025
				tooltip = experience_gain_army_factor_tt
			}
			custom_effect_tooltip = generic_skip_one_line_tt
			custom_effect_tooltip = generic_ft_get_more_generals_tt
		}
	}

	#Elite Army Principle
	focus = {
		id = generic_elite_army
		icon = GFX_focus_generic_urban_training
		prerequisite = { 
			focus = generic_army_effort
		}
		mutually_exclusive = {
			focus = generic_large_army
		}
		x = -4
		y = 1
		relative_position_id = generic_army_effort
		cost = 10

		search_filters = { FOCUS_FILTER_ARMY_XP }
		
		available_if_capitulated = yes

		completion_reward = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_dynamic_modifier = generic_army_spirit_red_dynamic
				}
				custom_effect_tooltip = GENERIC_modify_army_red_modifier_tt
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					has_dynamic_modifier = generic_army_spirit_revolutionary_dynamic
				}
				custom_effect_tooltip = GENERIC_modify_army_rev_modifier_tt
			}
			else = {
				custom_effect_tooltip = GENERIC_modify_army_modifier_tt
			}
			add_to_variable = { 
				army_spirit_experience_gain_army_unit_factor = 0.1
				tooltip = experience_gain_army_unit_factor_tt
			}
			add_to_variable = { 
				army_spirit_max_training = 0.10
				tooltip = max_training_tt
			}
			add_to_variable = { 
				army_spirit_training_time_army_factor = -0.05
				tooltip = training_time_army_factor_tt
			}
		}
		ai_will_do = {
			factor = 2
		}
	}

	#Army Doctrine Effort
	focus = {

					has_dynamic_modifier = generic_army_spirit_red_dynamic

				focus = generic_assimilation_effort

		}
		ai_will_do = {

		}
	}

	#Army War College
	focus = {
		id = generic_army_college
		icon = GFX_focus_chi_army_reform
		prerequisite = { focus = generic_doctrine_effort }
		x = -2
		y = 1
		relative_position_id = generic_doctrine_effort
		cost = 5

		search_filters = { FOCUS_FILTER_ARMY_XP FOCUS_FILTER_MILITARY_CHARACTER }
		
		available = {
			any_character = {
				is_army_leader = yes
			}
		}

		available_if_capitulated = yes

		completion_reward = {
			custom_effect_tooltip = generic_every_general_lvl_up
			every_army_leader = {
				limit = {
					skill < 2
				}
				add_skill_level = 1
			}
			custom_effect_tooltip = generic_skip_one_line_tt
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_dynamic_modifier = generic_army_spirit_red_dynamic
				}
				custom_effect_tooltip = GENERIC_modify_army_red_modifier_tt
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					has_dynamic_modifier = generic_army_spirit_revolutionary_dynamic
				}
				custom_effect_tooltip = GENERIC_modify_army_rev_modifier_tt
			}
			else = {
				custom_effect_tooltip = GENERIC_modify_army_modifier_tt
			}
			add_to_variable = { 
				army_spirit_army_leader_start_level = 1
				tooltip = army_leader_start_level_tt
			}
			add_to_variable = { 
				army_spirit_army_leader_cost_factor = -0.15
				tooltip = army_leader_cost_factor_tt
			}
			every_army_leader = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { has_trait = career_officer }
				}
				random_select_amount = 2
				add_trait = { trait = career_officer }
			}
		}
		ai_will_do = {
			factor = 2
		}
	}

	#Army Technical College

				tooltip = experience_loss_factor_tt
			}
			random_army_leader = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { 
						OR = {
							has_trait = career_officer
							has_trait = infantry_officer
							has_trait = cavalry_officer
							has_trait = armor_officer
							has_trait = engineer_officer
						}
					}
				}
				add_unit_leader_trait = engineer_officer 
			}
		}
		ai_will_do = {
			factor = 2
		}
	}

			air_experience = 25

				add_building_construction = {
					type = air_base
					level = 2
					instant_build = yes
				}
			}
			random_owned_controlled_state = {
				limit = {
					free_building_slots = {
						building = air_base
						size > 1
					}
				}
				add_building_construction = {
					type = air_base
					level = 1
					instant_build = yes
				}
			}
		}
		completion_reward = {
			air_experience = 25
			if = {
				limit = {
					capital_scope = {
						OR = {
							NOT = { is_controlled_by = ROOT }
							NOT = {
								free_building_slots = {
									building = air_base
									size > 1
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
				random_owned_controlled_state = {
					limit = {
						free_building_slots = {
							building = air_base
							size > 1
						}
					}
					add_building_construction = {
						type = air_base
						level = 2
						instant_build = yes
					}
					ROOT = { set_country_flag = aviation_effort_2_AB }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					capital_scope = {
						is_controlled_by = ROOT
						free_building_slots = {
							building = air_base
							size > 2
						}
					}
				}
				capital_scope = {
					add_building_construction = {
						type = air_base
						level = 2
						instant_build = yes
					}				
					ROOT = { set_country_flag = aviation_effort_2_AB }
				}
			}
			random_owned_controlled_state = {
				limit = {
					free_building_slots = {
						building = air_base
						size > 1
					}
				}
				add_building_construction = {
					type = air_base
					level = 1
					instant_build = yes
				}
			}
			custom_effect_tooltip = generic_skip_one_line_tt
			reduce_focus_completion_cost = {
				focus = generic_assimilation_effort
				cost = 35
			}
		}

						dockyard < 1
					}
				}
			}
		}

###Industry Focuses
	#Industrial Effort
	focus = {
		id = generic_industrial_effort
		icon = GFX_goal_generic_production
		x = 24
		y = 0
		cost = 5

		search_filters = { FOCUS_FILTER_INDUSTRY FOCUS_FILTER_RESEARCH }
		
		available_if_capitulated = yes

		completion_reward = {
			add_tech_bonus = {
				name = industrial_bonus
				bonus = 1.0
				uses = 2
				category = industry
			}
			custom_effect_tooltip = generic_skip_one_line_tt
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_dynamic_modifier = generic_industry_spirit_communist_dynamic
				}
				custom_effect_tooltip = GENERIC_modify_industry_com_modifier_tt
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					has_dynamic_modifier = generic_industry_spirit_fascist_dynamic
				}
				custom_effect_tooltip = GENERIC_modify_industry_fasc_modifier_tt
			}
			else = {
				add_dynamic_modifier = {
					modifier = generic_industry_spirit_dynamic
				}
			}
			add_to_variable = { 
				industry_spirit_production_factory_start_efficiency_factor = 0.05
				tooltip = production_factory_start_efficiency_factor_tt
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					capital_scope = {
						NOT = {
							is_on_continent = europe
						}
					}
				}
				add_to_variable = { 
					industry_spirit_industrial_capacity_factory = -0.01
					tooltip = industrial_capacity_factory_tt
				}
				add_to_variable = { 
					industry_spirit_consumer_goods_factor = 0.01
					tooltip = consumer_goods_factor_tt
				}
			}
		}

		ai_will_do = {
			factor = 3
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				date < 1937.1.1
				OR = { 
					# we dont want chinese minors to go crazy on slots early since they get eaten
					tag = GXC 
					tag = YUN
					tag = SHX
					tag = XSM
					tag = BEL
					tag = LUX
					tag = HOL
					tag = DEN

					# we also dont want tiny nations to go crazy with slots right away
					num_of_controlled_states < 2
				}				
			}
		}
	}

	#Dockyard Expansion
	focus = {
		id = expand_dockyards_focus
		icon = GFX_focus_generic_refit_civilian_ships
		prerequisite = { focus = generic_naval_effort }
		prerequisite = { focus = generic_industrial_effort }
		x = 2
		y = 1
		relative_position_id = generic_naval_effort
		cost = 10
		search_filters = {  FOCUS_FILTER_INDUSTRY }
		available_if_capitulated = no
		available = {
			any_state = {
				is_coastal = yes
				is_controlled_by = ROOT
			}
		}

		complete_tooltip = {
			add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 4
			add_building_construction = {
				type = dockyard
				level = 2
				instant_build = yes
			}
		}
		
		completion_reward = {
			random_owned_controlled_state = {
				limit = {
					is_coastal = yes
					free_building_slots = {
						building = dockyard
						size > 2
						include_locked = yes
					}
				}
				add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 2
				add_building_construction = {
					type = dockyard
					level = 2
					instant_build = yes
				}
			}
		}
		ai_will_do = {
			factor = 1
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				all_owned_state = {
					OR = {

					}
				}
			}
		}

	#Further Research Effort
	focus = {
		id = generic_extra_tech_slot_2
		icon = GFX_focus_research
		overlay = GFX_focus_fast_overlay_america 
		prerequisite = { 
			focus = generic_electronics
		}
		available = {
			OR= {
				num_of_factories > 50
				original_research_slots < 3
			}
		}
		cancel_if_invalid = no
		continue_if_invalid = yes
		x = 0
		y = 1

	#Shared Research Developments
	focus = {
		id = extra_tech_slot_3
		icon = GFX_focus_research
		prerequisite = { focus = generic_extra_tech_slot_2 }
		available = {
			has_war = yes
			is_in_faction = yes
			OR = {
				num_of_factories > 50
				any_other_country = {
					is_in_faction_with = ROOT
					num_of_factories > 50
				}
			}
		}		
		x = 0
		y = 2
		relative_position_id = generic_extra_tech_slot_2
		cost = 10
	
		search_filters = { FOCUS_FILTER_RESEARCH }

		available_if_capitulated = yes

		completion_reward = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					original_research_slots < 3
				}
				add_research_slot = 1
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					original_research_slots > 2
				}
				add_breakthrough_progress = {
					specialization = specialization_land
					value = 0.75
				}		
				add_breakthrough_progress = {
					specialization = specialization_air
					value = 0.75
				}
				add_breakthrough_progress = {
					specialization = specialization_naval
					value = 0.75
				}
			}			
		}
		ai_will_do = {
			factor = 3
		}
	}

###Politics
	#Internal Politics
	focus = {
		id = political_sphere
		icon = GFX_goal_generic_demand_territory
		x = 55
		y = 0
		cost = 5
		search_filters = { FOCUS_FILTER_POLITICAL }
		available_if_capitulated = yes
		offset = {
			x = -12
			y = 0
			trigger = {
				has_completed_focus = radicalism_focus
				has_game_rule = {
					rule = obsolete_focus_branches_visibility
					option = HIDE
				}
			}
		}
		offset = {
			x = -7
			y = 0
			trigger = {
				has_completed_focus = moderate_focus
				has_game_rule = {
					rule = obsolete_focus_branches_visibility
					option = HIDE
				}
			}
		}
		offset = {
			x = -10
			y = 0
			trigger = {
				has_completed_focus = sickle_sword_focus
				has_game_rule = {
					rule = obsolete_focus_branches_visibility
					option = HIDE
				}
			}
		}
		offset = {
			x = -4

	#Independant Communism
	focus = {
		id = independant_communism_focus
		icon = GFX_focus_SOV_support_spanish_poum
		prerequisite = { 
			focus = collectivisation_focus
			focus = revolutionary_socialism
		}
		mutually_exclusive = {
			focus = tow_the_moscow_line_focus
		}
		available = {
			
		}
		x = -1
		y = 2
		relative_position_id = revolutionary_socialism
		cost = 5

		search_filters = { FOCUS_FILTER_POLITICAL } 
		
		available_if_capitulated = yes

		completion_reward = {
			add_country_leader_trait = independent_communist
			if = {
				limit = {
					SOV = {
						has_government = communism
					}
				}
				SOV = {
					add_opinion_modifier = { target = ROOT modifier = generic_anti_soviet_communism }
					add_ai_strategy = {
						type = alliance
						id = ROOT
						value = -200
					}
					add_ai_strategy = {
						type = befriend
						id = ROOT
						value = -200
					}
					add_ai_strategy = {
						type = support
						id = ROOT
						value = -200
					}
				}
			}
			every_country = {
				limit = {
					NOT = {
						original_tag = SOV
					}
					has_government = communism
				}
				add_opinion_modifier = { target = ROOT modifier = generic_independant_communism_boost }
				add_ai_strategy = {
					type = alliance
					id = ROOT
					value = 50
				}
				add_ai_strategy = {
					type = befriend
					id = ROOT
					value = 100
				}
				add_ai_strategy = {
					type = support
					id = ROOT
					value = 200
				}
			}
		}

		ai_will_do = {
			factor = 5
		}
	}

###Fascist Path
	#Install Party Subordinates

		}
		available = {
			hidden_trigger = {
				OR = {
					is_ai = no
					AND = {
						is_ai = yes
						is_historical_focus_on = no
					}
					AND = {
						is_ai = yes
						has_government = neutrality
					}
				}
			}
		}
		offset = {
			x = -28
			y = 0
			trigger = {
				has_completed_focus = authoritarian_regime_focus
				has_game_rule = {
					rule = obsolete_focus_branches_visibility
					option = HIDE
				}
			}
		}
		allow_branch = {
			IF = {
				limit = {
					has_game_rule = {
						rule = obsolete_focus_branches_visibility
						option = HIDE
					}
				}
				NOT = { 
					OR = {
						has_completed_focus = moderate_focus
						has_completed_focus = radicalism_focus
					}
				}
			}
		}
		x = 28
		y = 2
		relative_position_id = political_sphere
		cost = 5

		search_filters = { FOCUS_FILTER_POLITICAL } 
		
		available_if_capitulated = yes

		completion_reward = {
			hidden_effect = { #collapse focus tree
				load_focus_tree = {
					tree = generic_focus_improved
					keep_completed = yes
				}
				mark_focus_tree_layout_dirty = yes
			}
			add_timed_idea = {
			    idea = generic_unaligned
			    days = 1095
			}
		}

		ai_will_do = {
			factor = 100
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				AND = {
					is_historical_focus_on = yes
					OR = {
						has_country_leader_ideology = moderatism
						has_country_leader_ideology = centrism
						NOT = {
							has_government = neutrality
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
